# Overnight build log, 2026-11-14

This is the unattended overnight run that builds the chunk tasks the owner's sheet answers settle on their own.
Every package has a restore point at `refs/overnight/<id>-before`, and the whole run starts from `refs/overnight/start`.
Nothing was committed, pushed or stashed. The tree stays uncommitted on purpose.
Open new questions, conflict rows and play sessions were not treated as answers.

## Morning report

18 packages ran. All 18 were verified, none was kept with open problems, none was reverted. The tree is green
against the baseline: the final runs fail only the baseline's three required GameTests (see "Final check" at the end
of this section).

Nothing was committed. Every package section below has its own Built, Skipped, Tests, "For the owner" and
Verification blocks; this section is the short version.

### How to undo

Nothing was committed, pushed or stashed. `refs/overnight/start` is the tree before the run. `refs/overnight/<id>-before`
(P01 to P18) is the tree before each package. Diff a package with `git diff refs/overnight/P07-before refs/overnight/P08-before`
for a finished one, or `git diff refs/overnight/P18-before` for the last. The refs hold tracked files only: untracked
files a package changed were copied under `build/overnight/<id>-untracked/` or `build/overnight/<id>-backup/` where
the log says so, and new files are listed in each package's head.

## How to verify

Run each command from the repo root, one at a time. Only one Gradle build may run in this tree at once.

| Suite | Command | Result line | Time tonight |
|---|---|---|---|
| Compile and JVM unit suite | `./gradlew.bat compileJava compileTestJava compileGameTestJava test --rerun --offline --console=plain` | `BUILD SUCCESSFUL` at the end of the Gradle output. Counts come from `build/test-results/test/*.xml` (sum `tests`, `failures`, `errors`). Failures also print as `FAILED` lines in the Gradle output. | 1.8 min |
| Combined GameTest run | `./gradlew.bat runGameTestServer --offline --console=plain` | `build/phlegethontic-gametest/logs/latest.log`, after `GAME TESTS COMPLETE`: "All N required tests passed :)" or "N required tests failed :(" followed by the names. A required failure fails the Gradle build. | 2.2 min |
| Playthrough suite | `./gradlew.bat runPlaythroughGameTestServer --offline --console=plain` | `build/playthrough-gametest/logs/latest.log`: "All 427 required tests passed :)" then "98 optional tests failed" and the optional names. Optional failures do not fail the build. | 2.6 min |
| One named suite | `./gradlew.bat run<Suite>GameTestServer --offline --console=plain`, the launcher named in `gradle/game-tests.gradle` (for example `runBloodInjectionGameTestServer`, `runRiteKindGameTestServer`, `runDialogueRouteGameTestServer`) | `<suite directory>/logs/latest.log`, same summary lines. The directory is the suite's `directory` in the `gameTestSuites` table. | not timed tonight; a small suite is about 1 to 2 min |

### Skipped

| Task or part | Why |
| --- | --- |
| T7, the `CardinalRiteSync` site | The plan's premise is wrong. A level with no active rite already sends nothing (the rite tick returns before the sync). With a rite running, every entry carries its phase ticks, so the payload changes every half second and a diff would skip nothing; `RiteEntry` also has no equality. Not worth the risk to the countdown display. FX-14 is therefore built in part and not recorded. |
| S-5b widened to `EarlyInitiation.give` | First built in `give`, which the initiation starter kit and the Mortal Display lodestone also use. That flipped the optional playthrough probe `creativeFullInventoryStarterKit`, which is the open playthrough decision D-16 (Creative discard of the starter kit). Narrowed to the Concentrated Blood vial only; `give` is back as it was. |
| Ledger APPROVED for G-1/F-18, F-16, F-17, G-2b | Combat rows belong to the combat package. |
| AGENTS.md documentation contract (kits and Scale Grip as listed claims) | Optional in the plan; AGENTS.md was left alone. |
| `docs/audits/2026-11-11-visual-balance/particles-and-texels.md` OBJ note | The plan asks for it, but `docs/README.md` says dated audit snapshots are never edited. Left as it was. |
| Records for FX-23, FX-24 | Built in the baseline step, not this package; FX-24's clean task waits on NQ-17-5. |
| Black Heart lit-state change test | Lighting the heart needs the Blackhearted passive learned and active; the test covers the unlit case and a new player object instead. |

### For the owner

- No new or changed player text, no new lang keys.
- F-15 changes play: a living weapon with dormant blood is now free, and a short wielder keeps their blood and loses the weapon. Before, they were emptied first.
- The Scale Grip return now waits for the living player and never drops: a respawn with a full pack holds the item until there is room.
- `creativeFullInventoryStarterKit` (D-16) is still open. While building S-5b I saw the same one-line change in `EarlyInitiation.give` would settle it if you choose "treat a Creative discard as undelivered".
- Plan premise wrong: rite sync is not sent while a level has no rite (see Skipped). The rite site would need a distance filter, not a diff, to save much.
- Flight state is kept per player object rather than by UUID (the plan said a UUID map). That way a respawn, and the client's own copy in single player, start clean, and a stale grant cannot leave a survival player flying.
- No review client was needed for this package.

## P03. Cast economy: cost floor, refunds, single-cast fixes, Temper and regeneration numbers

Restore point: `refs/overnight/P03-before`. Plan: chunk 04 (`04-cast-economy-and-temper.md`).

### Built

| Task | Rulings | What changed, with the owner's values | Main files |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| T1 (part) | LP-6 | The discounts in a cost ledger together never go below 0.4x (`ManipulationCostRules.DISCOUNT_FLOOR`). A `discount_floor` line (key `message.hemomancy.manipulation_cost_ledger.discount_floor`, "Discount floor") lifts them back, so the Scrying tooltip shows it and the diagnostics payload keeps its layout. Surcharges still multiply on top. A zero line (purity 0) stays blocking and is never lifted. | `ManipulationCostRules.java` |
| T1 (part) | LP-7 | Exsanguinate returns 60% of the blood the cast paid, on a kill. Grave Debt stores the blood paid for the mark (`hemomancy:grave_debt_paid`, beside the owner id) and returns 60% of it only when the death's kill credit is its caster. A Drudge's or entity's mark stores 0, so a Drudge's Grave Debt no longer pays its owner. Both refunds go through `BloodFlowLedger.applyDirectIncome` (sources `exsanguinate_refund`, `grave_debt_refund`). The paid amount reaches the cast through `BloodManipulation.paidForCurrentCast()`, set only around `getAction` in `tryPerformAction`, so a Crawling Choir echo or a direct `getAction` refunds nothing. | `BloodManipulation.java`, `ExsanguinateManip.java`, `GraveDebtManip.java`, `SchoolHitHelper.java`, `ManipulationStatusRules.java`, `EntityCasts.java`, `DrudgeActions.java` |
| T2 | CB-6 | `MIN_CHARGE_FRACTION` 0.25 to 0.5. | `ManipulationCastingRules.java` |
| T2 | CB-8 | Osseous Bloom deals at most 20 to a non-boss over 100 max health; bosses keep 12. One `OsseousBloomManip.damageCap`, read by the cast and by `SchoolCombatEvents` after affinity. | `OsseousBloomManip.java`, `SchoolCombatEvents.java` |
| T2 | CB-9 | Hematic Beacon heals 0.8 a pulse (was 0.4), `HEMATIC_BEACON_HEAL_PER_PULSE`. | `ManipulationReactiveEvents.java` |
| T2 | CB-10 | Endless Hour's cooldown is 1,200 ticks and starts again when the hour settles (expiry or logout), through the new `BloodManipulation.restartCooldown`. Cooldown reductions apply as to any cooldown. | `EndlessHourManip.java`, `ManipulationInit.java`, `BloodManipulation.java` |
| T3 (part) | LP-13 | Scar refill 0.25 to 0.1 per second per tier. | `TemperRules.java` |
| T3 (part) | CQ-16 | Bone block 24 to 9. `CarrionRules.temper` never goes above a remain's worth for any remain (skulls and bone blocks were multiplied by the work scale); a dry pulse still gives less. | `CarrionRules.java` |
| T3 (part) | CB-19 | `TemperCatalysts.find` takes the shortfall and spends the smallest carried catalyst that covers it; the off hand wins a tie, then the earlier slot. When none covers, the charge is dry, as before. | `TemperCatalysts.java`, `TemperCharge.java` |
| T3 (part) | CB-25 | A swap into a Staff weapon form burns 1 Temper (`LivingStaffWeaponFormRules.HOT_SWAP_TEMPER`) in the form's primary school, planned and settled by `TemperCharge` like any charge, so a dry swap pays the swap blood x1.25 (the CL-10 cast rate).   Fix: report a sink take as a tank transfer only when the sink holds the blood (for example a `holdsProjectedBlood()`
  default on `ProjectedBloodSink`, false for those two), and add a test that spent projection still grows rings.
  The log line "Tank projection is the `ProjectedBloodSink` branch" should then say which sinks count.

  owner under "For the owner", so I count it as a note.

  P03 changes and the ref cannot put them back. I judged them from the files as they are.

### Fix (round 1)

- Fixed the blocking CQ-8 problem. `BloodProjectionItem.performProjection` now reports a sink take as a tank transfer
  only when the sink keeps the blood: new `BloodProjectionItem.holdsProjectedBlood(sink)`, true only for an
  `IBloodReservoir` whose existing `holdsBlood()` is true. So the Ring Door, the Monolith gate and the Mason's Effigy
  (sinks that are not reservoirs), and the Somatic Loom and the Covenant Throne (reservoirs with no tank), grow rings
  again. A filler answers as its main block does. I used the existing `holdsBlood()` rather than a new method on
  `ProjectedBloodSink`, so no file outside this package changed. Only `BloodProjectionItem.java` changed in main code.
- Added the missing test: `StaffMiningGameTests.onlyATankStopsProjectionGrowingRings`. A scriptorium counts as a tank;
  the Ring Door, Monolith, Mason's Effigy and Somatic Loom sinks do not; the Mason's Effigy and Heartwood core
  endpoints answer `holdsProjectedBlood()` false and the Bloodwell true. It checks the classification, not a full
  projection into the Ring Door or Monolith, whose opening needs an Archon path and an arena.
- Corrected the Built row for T7 and the CQ-8 owner note above, `docs/archive/tracks/core_loop/08-living-staff-and-mining.md` and the
  Staff rings line in `docs/HEMOMANCY_REFERENCE.md`.
- Taken out: nothing. CB-25's x1.25 dry swap stays as built and flagged for the owner.
- Runs: unit suite `BUILD SUCCESSFUL in 2m 14s`, 5,981 tests, 0 failures, 0 errors
  (`build/overnight/P03-fix1-unit.txt`). Combined run "1858 GAME TESTS COMPLETE", "3 required tests failed": the
  baseline three with the same first differences (line 1591, line 33, fingerprint line 200); 1,858 is the 1,857 before
  plus the new test (`build/overnight/P03-fix1-gametest-latest.log`). No Java process left running.
- The untracked-file restore point note stands. I did not change any of those files except `StaffMiningGameTests.java`
  (the new test).

### Fix (round 1)

## P07. Saints and mausoleums

Restore point: `refs/overnight/P07-before`. Plan: chunk 08 (`08-saints-and-mausoleums.md`). The status before is
`build/overnight/P07-status-before.txt`. Three files are new and untracked (listed under Built), and
`tools/model_export/putriciel.py` is tracked, so the ref holds its old version.

### Built

| Task | Rulings | What changed, with the owner's values | Main files |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| T1 | C-6 | Canon recipes need D8 at the Loom and at the learn step. `MemoryWeavingRecipe` has an optional `required_degree` (absent is 0), read and written by the serializer's JSON, its `KEYS` and its stream codec. The four canon recipes carry `"required_degree":  8` (their two-space style kept). The Loom refuses a weaver below it in `tryChargeRitualBlood`, server side, before the engram check, so no blood, span or thread is taken; it reuses `message.hemomancy.somatic_loom_block_entity.requires_degree` and compares through `ProgressionGates.hasDegree`. - New lines to read (narration, no Saint speaks):
  - `message.hemomancy.chapel.beholding.hemorath.1` "The first student fills the basins, so the vessel drinks from stone and not from them."
  - `.hemorath.2` "The second sets the iron bands while he still swells. They creak shut over his ribs."
  - `.hemorath.3` "The third lays the lid. She watches his hands the whole time, as she was taught."
  - `.hemorath.4` "The last seals the seam with his own blood, then steps back before it can be taken."
  - `.hemorath.done` "His students sealed him because they feared becoming him. That fear outlived them and became the Order's discipline."
  - `.seraphae.1` "Her hand on a lame man's knee. He stands, and the knee keeps growing."
  - `.seraphae.2` "Her hand on a burned child's arm. The skin closes, and does not stop closing."
  - `.seraphae.3` "Her hand on a fevered woman's brow. The fever breaks, and her nerves begin to fire on their own."
  - `.seraphae.4` "Her hand on an old lord who should have died years ago. He lives, and swells, and splits."
  - `.seraphae.done` "Four touches, four growths. Her own people chained her here, where she could reach no one."
  - `message.hemomancy.chapel.cooldown` "The memory has not settled yet."

## P08. Combat rules: characterisation, G-2b and F-16

Restore point: `refs/overnight/P08-before`. Plan: chunk 17 (`17-architecture-combat-rules-and-housekeeping.md`), tasks
T9, T10 and T11. The status before is `build/overnight/P08-status-before.txt`. Every file this package edited is tracked,
so the ref holds its old version. The three new files are listed under Built.

### Built

| Task | Rulings | What changed, with the owner's values | Main files |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| T9 | G-2b, F-16 (and G-1/F-18, F-17 for later) | Characterisation only, written and seen green on the tree before any behaviour change. The targeting matrix gained a row for each of the 13 missing wider-preset sites (Silver Rebuke, Still Pulse, Memory Shear, Quietus Bell, the Saprovitta trail, Sheolic retribution, Crimson Wake, the Pale Silver Bell, the Sporitic Thurible aura, Predatory Eyes, Phantasmal Masquerade's hostile check, the Rite of the Closed Vein, a Drudge's Activation Potential). It also gained a kill-credit row for each of the four damaging F-16 sites (all "none" before). Two new goldens: the damage stages (20 rows: no modifier for five hit kinds, 13 modifiers alone, two stacks, a hit zeroed at LOW) and the death-saves (the six saves alone and in pairs, 22 rows; Ink Mantle with Cryptobiosis is one Morphling slot, so that line reads "cannot arm together"). Each line says how its modifier was armed. To reach Predatory Eyes from a test, its gather moved into `ThelemicMemoryWorldEvents.predatoryEyesPrey` (no behaviour change). The Thurible aura and the Closed Vein rite are called through reflection, as they stand. | `CombatTargetingMatrixGameTests.java`, new `CombatStageCharacterisationGameTests.java`, new `src/gameTest/resources/combat/damage-stages.txt` and `death-saves.txt`, `ThelemicMemoryWorldEvents.java` |
| T10 | G-2b | `Reach.MONSTER_FILTER` and `MONSTER_FILTER_ANY_STATE` have `allies = false`: the caster's own puppets, pets, team-mates' monsters and impressed mobs no longer match a Monster filter. The two Drudge lookups (`DrudgeActions.monsters` and `activationPotential`) ask `canHarmFor(drudge, monster)`. That applies the owner's strict rule (team and `canAttack` when the owner is away) and also stops them reaching dying bodies and Blood Constructs. The Glacier scar asks the preset for a player wearer and `canHarmFor` for any other wearer. The plan was right that the flag alone would throw at these three sites. | `ManipulationCombatHelper.java`, `DrudgeActions.java`, `ScarDefinition.java` |
| T11 | F-16 | Silent Severance, Autoimmune Edge, Silver Rebuke, the Saprovitta trail and Sheolic Crimson Retribution ask the strict `canHarm`. Retribution gained the check; it still acts only on its attacker. The four damaging sites hit with `damageSources().indirectMagic(player, player)` instead of an unowned `magic()`, so the kill counts for the player. No damage number, cost or cooldown changed. `Reach.UNGATED_SURVIVAL` had no user left and is gone. `Reach.UNGATED` keeps the morphling look target. The source is not a melee type, so `SchoolDamage.wrap` never gives it a held weapon's school. | `SilentArchonArmorAbilityHandler.java`, `StillArtInit.java`, `ScarsEntityEventHandler.java`, `SheolicBastionBloodlustArmorAbilityHandler.java`, `ManipulationCombatHelper.java` |

### For the owner

- No new or changed player text, no lang keys.
- Play changes. Your pets, puppets, team-mates' monsters and mobs you impressed are safe from every Monster-filter effect. A Drudge no longer attacks its owner's puppets. Silent Severance, Autoimmune Edge, Silver Rebuke, the Saprovitta trail and Crimson Retribution no longer touch pets, allies, your puppets or a PvP-off player. Their kills now pay you blood, Temper, skill points and purity.
- A Drudge whose owner is offline or in another dimension falls back to team and vanilla `canAttack`, so it can hit the owner's puppets then. That is the plan's reading of `canHarmFor`.
- The goldens show things T13 will change. Worth reading before you answer NQ-17-1 and NQ-17-2 (`src/gameTest/resources/combat/`):
  - A hit zeroed late still costs its full blood (50 mL) and vein strain. So does a hit the Husk Effigy zeroes.
  - Penumbral ambush multiplies after blood and strain are read: 15 taken, 50 mL lost.
  - Silent Refusal fires before Endless Hour. Its line shows 3,200 mL: its 3,000 price plus the 200 mL the lethal hit
    drains as a wound.
  - With Endless Hour running, Ink Mantle and Cryptobiosis never fire: the hour holds the player at 1 health first.
  - The Curved Horn saves with no Last Rite cooldown (F-17, T12). With Silent Refusal or a Morphling save armed, those fire first and the Horn takes no wear.
  - The strongest stack recorded (Iron Will with the avatar) takes 5.25 of 10.
- The Phantasmal row reads the echoes, not the check itself: with a monster near, four of the eight echoes come out aggressive instead of two.
- `AGENTS.md` still calls the `Reach.STRICT` move a pending decision. One clause needs to go when you next edit it.
- Not seen in a client. Two clients would show it best (the plan's "Still to do": pets, puppets and a PvP-off player spared).
- No game server, client or Gradle task is left running.

- Scope: the diff from `refs/overnight/P10-before` and the status compare match the brief and the log. Only the listed

  built. PT-5 was put back: `sanguine_monolith.json`, `WorkingCraftRosterDataTest` and `PlaythroughAdeptGameTests` are
  unchanged. Nothing for PT-11, PT-18, PT-26, PT-27, PT-41, T2, T4, T5, T8 or T10. No lash, held-form, living HUD, vat or
  Vigil model file was touched. No new line uses "Shaping". No payload, particle list, allow-list or guardrail changed.
- Values checked in code: a craft that closes with its crafter offline owes `STRUCTURE_CRAFT`, and a Loom climax with no
  weaver owes the weave; `OfflineCredits.onLogin` applies each credit once through the appliers table, not
  `PlayerDataMigrations`. The template has a lit campfire at (8, 2, 5) under the Alembic at (8, 3, 5); I decoded both
  versions of `alchemist.nbt` and that is the only cell that changed. `isHeatSource` takes a lit campfire. The jar
  lesson decorator adds the card from D3 on, still behind `ClinicalBloodKnowledge.eligible` (no purifying or clarity
  player). Only the timeout and the paired chair exit call `returnFromVisit(player, true)`; logout, death protection,
  the corridor gate, the admin toggle and leaving the dimension all go through the `false` path. The rumour and scrap
  are in the D5, D6, D7 and D8 Vicar trees and the handler takes `hasDegree(5)`; the real packet path still needs a
  Vicar (`DialogueEventSpeakers`). The Cornerstone line shows only while it is unclaimed. Each `CovenantProgress` step
  has a lang key for its card.
- Tests: every built task except PT-24 has a running test that fails without it. Each flipped probe went from optional
  to required and was rewritten to the ruling; none was deleted or made optional.

  `afalteringwillstilltakestheprojection` in `ShapingValidationGameTests` ("Start element ... is larger than end
  element", an entity query range overflow at test x 14,556,977). P10 touches no projection, Will or Shaping code, the
  error appears in no earlier log in `build/`, and run 2 passed it: "1900 GAME TESTS COMPLETE", the baseline three only.

    `guidedChamberLogoutAndLethalDamage` need a world where the Chamber loads. The log says so.

    and the commit happens at a lit Iron Brazier (ledger `vein_mason.commit_loadout`). AGENTS.md keeps preparation and
    commit apart. The voice pass (chunk 16) could word it as "prepared at a Mason's Effigy".

### Fix (round 1)

Both blocking problems were fixed. Nothing was taken out.

- `ClinicalBloodProgressionGameTests.craftedBlankCarriesWovenVesselIntoFirstLoomWeave` rewritten to PT-3: nothing drops
  at the Mnemonist, the pack gains exactly one Bleeding Bulb and three Vivacious Enzymes, and nothing stays owed. It no
  longer picks the kit up off the ground.
- `HarbingerJourneyAutomation`, `VICAR_REWARD` stage: the `touchDrop` of Hematic Iron Scrap is gone. The stage now only
  chooses the Vicar's reward option, and the kit lands in the pack through the claim. `HemoJourneyFixtures` already
  counts the pack for this stage and for the other reward stages, so it needed no change. The other reward stages
  (Hermit Road report, Worn Vow, Woven Vessel, Vein-Mason first lesson) never picked up a drop. This also mends the
  `/hemo test` journey runs at the Vicar reward. `journeyCompletesMissingFirstBloodcraftProofsBeforeVicar` only had
  its message reworded ("attributable to the pack").
- Non-blocking, tests: seven new GameTests in `HarbingerRepairGameTests`, one per converted NPC reward:
  `fullPackOwesFirstBloodcraftKitThenLandsOnce`, `...HermitRoadAsh...`, `...MasonsRespiteMap...`,
  `...MnemonistStarterMemory...`, `...WovenVesselKit...`, `...WornVowScrap...`, `...VeinMasonFirstLessonKit...`. Each
  earns the reward through the real dialogue route with every pack slot taken, then checks: nothing drops, the exact
  stacks are owed, a dead player gets nothing, a Creative full pack gets nothing, part of the reward lands when only
  part fits (the rest stays owed), and the whole reward lands exactly once when there is room. The map and the starter
  memory are one single item, so they have no partial case. Save, load and the death copy stay covered by the shared
  `owedNpcRewardWaitsUntilItReallyLandsThenArrivesOnce`, since all seven use the same attachment. The starter kit keeps
  its `fullInventoryInitiationOwesStarterBloodOnlyOnce` (full and partial) and the shared test (dead, Creative).
- Non-blocking, docs: the PT-28 row in the Built table now says what the fingerprints show. A forged event from another
  speaker shows the refusal line for the church map and the Vein-Mason D5 and D6 rewards; the packet path still filters
  speakers.
- Left as they were, for the owner: PT-22 counts only the section the cast strains (an owner word is needed on whether
  any Varicose section should count); the PT-14 lesson keys stay in `getPersistentData()` under `PlayerPersisted`
  beside the route's existing keys. Moving them to an attachment is a larger change than this fix round.
- Files changed in this round: `ClinicalBloodProgressionGameTests.java`, `journey/HarbingerJourneyAutomation.java`,
  `HarbingerRepairGameTests.java`, this log.

## P12. Bloodline pool and small dialogue fixes

Restore point: `refs/overnight/P12-before`. Plans: chunk 11 (`11-bloodline-and-throne.md`) T5 and T9, chunk 16
(`16-npc-dialogue-canon-and-liber.md`) T1, T2 and part of T7. The status before is `build/overnight/P12-status-before.txt`.
No file was created; every edited file is tracked, so the ref holds its old version.

### Built

| Task | Rulings | What changed, with the owner's values | Main files |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 11 T5 | PT-38 | The first Hematic Unbinding warns at activation, before the Nether Star is taken. The leader's first activation says the warning, takes no offering and starts nothing. A second activation within `HematicUnbindingRules.CONFIRMATION_TICKS` (12,000 ticks, ten minutes, as built) starts the rite, and its completion dissolves the line with no second warning. One Nether Star in all. The window is a session map keyed by player UUID in `HematicUnbindingRules` (`activate`, `clearSessionState`), cleared by `TransientServerStateEvents` at server start and stop, not on logout (a timer that outlasts a relog, as the hub's rule says). The completion-time warning and its three root `getPersistentData()` calls are gone. | `HematicUnbindingRules.java`, `CardinalRiteActivation.java`, `CardinalRiteCompletionEffects.java`, `TransientServerStateEvents.java`, `en_us.json`, `persistent-data-allow-list.txt` |
| 11 T9 | PT-42 | No "pool is full" line. The pool screen's Donate button is greyed every frame while the pool is at or above its maximum (`BloodlinePoolScreen.poolIsFull`, read from `BloodlinePoolClientData`). The fault the plan folds in is fixed: `PacketLumpDonate` clamps the donation to the pool's room before it pays (`BloodVolumeTransferRules.donationAmount(requested, donorBlood, poolRoom)`) and does nothing, silently, at zero room. Before, a donation into a full pool took the whole request from the player and the blood was lost. Projection on the Bloodwell already clamped to the room and is unchanged. | `BloodlinePoolScreen.java`, `PacketLumpDonate.java`, `BloodVolumeTransferRules.java` |
| 16 T1 | CD-5 | Annetta's line goes to the player. `hemomancy.annetta.draught.truth.line1` is removed from her `truth` node, from `en_us.json` and from the brief; the node opens on her stage direction (`truth.line2`) and her answer (`truth.line3`). The player's option `hemomancy.dialogue.annetta.option.what_is_it` carries the sense. | `AnnettaDialogueTrees.java`, `en_us.json`, `dialogue_briefs/lady_fungal_wanderers.json` |
| 16 T2 | FX-15 | "Fit or wrap", read as fit: each card is sized to its wrapped text, nothing is cut. Trade cards: the body is 54 px plus 9 px for every title line past one and every card line past two (`DialogueTradeLayout.bodyHeight`); `DialogueTradeView` draws every title and card line. Hub cards: the four cards grow to the most lines any summary wraps to (`DialogueHubLayout.calculate(shell, summaryLines)`), the summary draws in full, and the hub scrolls on a short window. Topic cards (Quest Work, Conversation, the Important tab): a card grows 12 px per summary line past two (`DialogueTopicCardLayout.height`). | `DialogueTradeLayout.java`, `DialogueTradeView.java`, `DialogueHubLayout.java`, `DialogueTopicCardLayout.java`, `DialogueScreen.java` |
| 16 T7 (part) | C-7, CD-2, CD-9, CD-11 | C-7: the Annetta encounter is promoted from Partial to "Implemented, balance pass pending" in `HEMOMANCY_REFERENCE.md` (the Annetta status entry) and `LORE_REFERENCE.md` (the status note and §11 "Implementation status"). CD-2, CD-9 and CD-11 were already true in the text (checked in `en_us.json` and the three `clairaudiograph.json` inquiries); only the rulings were recorded. | docs only |

- The NpcTradeReview save keeps rapport between runs, so its "board reads Trusted" check fails on this and every later run. A fresh `create` (or resetting the Alchemist's rapport in the stage step) would fix the harness. Not changed here.
- No payload changed, so `PROTOCOL_VERSION` stays. No vanilla particle, no new `getPersistentData()` call, no guardrail list raised. No Gradle task, server or client is left running.

## P14. The Brined Court

The status before is `build/overnight/P14-status-before.txt`, after is `P14-status-after.txt`. No file was created; every edited file is tracked, so the ref holds its old version.

| T1 | PT-45 | Standing no longer depends on the order of acts. The first visit-kind act (the site visit or the welcome gift) after the house's custom made the guest makes a friend; the custom still raises one step; two visit-kind acts alone stop at guest. The kind of an act already counted is read from its id (`CourtStandingRules.kindOf`: `custom*` is a custom, anything else a visit; `CourtProgress.anyActCounted`). `afterTransgression`, `afterAmends` and `afterLeaving` are unchanged (RV-79). The playthrough arrange act `playthrough_custom` is now `custom_playthrough` so its id reads as a custom. | `CourtStandingRules.java`, `CourtProgress.java`, `CourtLife.java`, `PlaythroughStates.java` |
| T1 | PT-46 | Leaving a usurpation costs the same one-day wait as dying (`USURP_RETRY_TICKS`, 24,000, as built). A logout ends the fight at once through a new `KindledChampionTrial.onLogout` (`PlayerLoggedOutEvent`), recorded while the player still exists. A challenger out of range or in another dimension past the 100-tick grace is now passed to `end(GONE, player)` when online anywhere, and `end` records the wait for `GONE` as for a death. Both say the new system line `hemomancy.court.champion.forfeit`. A dropped connection costs the day too, as the plan says follows from the ruling. | `KindledChampionEntity.java`, `KindledChampionTrial.java`, `KindledChampionRules.java` (comment), `CourtLife.java`, `en_us.json` |

Problems (none blocking):
- other: the PT-46 gap is wider than the log says. `KindledChampionTrial.onLogout` finds the fight with `facing`, which only searches a box of twice the leash round the player. A challenger who swims past that range in the same dimension and logs out inside the 100-tick grace is not charged: the tick then finds no online challenger and ends `GONE` with a null player. Fix: in `onLogout`, look for any `KindledChampionEntity` in the player's level (or every level) whose `isFacing(player)` is true, with no box.
- docs: "For the owner" names only "changes dimension and then logs out" for that gap. It also covers swimming out of range in the same dimension.
- missing-test: `escapingAUsurpationCostsTheDay` fires `firePlayerLoggedOut` by hand before taking the player offline, so a real disconnect is not exercised, and no test checks that `champion.forfeit` is said. The new JVM and GameTest cases were written with the code, not seen red first, as the log says.
- other: the three-argument `MermaidsPurseRules.swimBonus` was deleted in place rather than kept under `removed_assets/`. It is one method in a kept file and the restore point holds it, so this reads as an ordinary edit.
- No Gradle task, server or client is left running.

  - `hemomancy.mnemonist.circus.teaser.line2` (new): "I write no waybill to them before the fourth Degree. If you find one of their camps first, the Acrobat and the Dagger Thrower teach their bodies at the third."

Verdict: pass. The tree is green against the baseline and no problem is blocking. Checked against `git diff refs/overnight/P15-before` and `build/overnight/P15-status-before.txt` (58 tracked files changed; one file created, the `troupe_abandoned_stage_targets` tag; the four `removed_assets/` copies and their `MANIFEST.txt` rows are there).

- Scope: only T1, T2, T4, T5, T6 and the T19 records are built. No T3 gate, no T7 to T18 work, no yield scene or music, no Thread Ripper price change, no Hematic Lantern recolour, no Sculpting rename. Nothing in the lash, held forms, `bloodhud`, vats or Vigil was touched; `BloodNeedleGeometry` and `BloodProjectileEffects` only gain a troupe-knife branch. D-36, RV-16, RV-17, D-18, D-29, D-30, D-31, D-33, D-37 to D-39 and D-41 to D-44 are still open in `OPEN_QUESTIONS.md`.
- Values: refusal keys by outcome (`busy` for NEUTRAL, `settled` otherwise), `no_route` off Succession and `not_ready` on it; the ledger line is the plan's text; Peaceful refused in `begin` before anything spawns and ended in `tick`; `targetsNeeded` 2 for Mummer and Bellows, 1 otherwise; TEASER only at Degree 3, Waybill still from Degree 4; stage mode on `testimony.circus_understudy` and not `clue.stage`, not while sneaking; `actingFor` resolves player, projectile owner, Blood Cur owner and non-trial bound owner; `protectedBody` adds only the Vesper check; `vacant` saved only when true, set in `remove` (destroying reasons) and `die`, read by `isCompletedNear`. `carousel.png`: the 2,059 gold pixels (three shades) are gone, replaced by iron and bone; no other pixel changed. Pavilion NBT: only the two lantern columns moved, from x 28 and 36 to x 24 and 40 at y 5, chains to the roof; size and the 10 entities unchanged.
- Tests: each built task has a case that fails without it, except two parts of T6 (below). The flipped `mnemonistWovenTreeAtD3` and the six promoted probes assert the rulings, not weaker versions. `CircusIntroductionRulesTest` now runs (it was a `main` that never ran).
- Unit suite: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL in 1m 40s`, 6,029 tests, 0 failures, 0 errors (`build/overnight/P15-verify1-unit.txt`).
- Combined run: 1,917 tests, the baseline three required failures only; no optional failure. The fingerprint diff against the fresh actual file is lines 200, 304 and 307 only, the same file as the implementer's (`P15-verify1-gametest-latest.log`, `P15-verify1-route-actual.txt`).
- `runCrimsonTroupeGameTestServer`: "All 37 required tests passed :)" (`P15-verify1-crimson.txt`).
- Playthrough: "All 432 required tests passed :)", 60 optional failures, none outside the baseline and the same list as the implementer's (`P15-verify1-playthrough-optional.txt`). The promoted probes log what they should (stage tag true, 0 threats left and the retry line, vacant true, the D3 teaser keys).
- Client: `runCircusReviewClient -PcircusReviewOnly=performers,lanterns,liberation` with `{"op":"create","name":"CircusP15V1","seed":12345}`. It got past the Accessibility screen (the world was created and every scene ran), logged `CIRCUS_REVIEW captured 2609` with no exception and closed itself. The implementer's 1,888 frames were moved to `build/circus-review/screenshots-P15-impl/` first. Kept frames in `build/overnight/P15-verify1-shots/`: `performers-00370.png` (the Fire Breather's cone, bone edge readable on the red floor), `performers-01054.png` (a large knife with its wake and the three fan lines), `performers-01120.png` (the Strongman's blow with its wedge), `liberation-00200.png` (the carousel's dark iron pole, bone trim and crimson canopy, no lantern in front, the sever line low on screen).
- Guardrails: no payload changed, so `PROTOCOL_VERSION` and the payload registry stay. Every new line is an `en_us.json` key with a brief where its speaker has a briefs file. No blood is paid. The `CircusDemonstrations` vanilla-particle row left honestly; the Vesper persistent-data count rose by the one new reader. The deleted lang keys and briefs are in `removed_assets/`, and nothing in code still builds the bark keys.

Problems (none blocking):
- missing-test: two T6 parts have no automated check: the troupe knife putting the arrow count back (`CircusKnifeProjectileEntity.onHitEntity`) and the follow slots (`BoundSummonBehavior.followSlot`). A GameTest that hits a player with a troupe knife and reads `getArrowCount()`, and a JVM or GameTest case for the slot offsets, would pin them.
- wrong-value (check in PR-10): FX-27's answer says "hematic iron and crimson". The old gold became iron on the pole and frame and bone on the trim and finial (the plan's "crimson and bone"); the canopy was already crimson. No gold is left, so the no-brass rule holds, but the bone trim is the plan's reading, not the owner's word.
- other: the fan's 18 degrees, the 3-tick gap, the 2x knife size and the slot spacing (2.5 and 4.5 blocks back, 1.6 apart) are the builder's picks for "bolder" and "fix". They are visual tuning inside FX-25 and FX-28, not numbers for an open question, but the owner has not seen them.
- other: `followOwnerOnFoot` runs its 24-block entity query every tick while a body's navigation is done (the 10-tick gate only applies while it walks). Small cost per puppet; gating the query on the 10-tick beat as well would remove it.
- other: in `CircusPracticalController.begin` the Peaceful refusal comes before the Degree check, so a player below the lesson's Degree in Peaceful hears the Peaceful line, not the Degree line.
- docs: the knife wake is tinted bone in code but reads crimson in frames, as the log already says. The log is otherwise accurate.
- No Gradle task, server or client is left running.

### For the owner

- Every count, size and speed is the audit's starting value (PR-26). Shots: `build/summon-particle-review/screenshots/` and `build/ecology-particle-review/screenshots/`.
- The Echo burst reads well: a dark Tenebris cloud with purple motes (summon shot 1).
- The Luminal Cicada flash does not show in its shot, though the log says the flash ran. The hit glow is the audit's "real focal flash"; in daylight it does not read in a still, as P17 found for the Silver Dawn hit glow. Worth a look in motion or at night.
- The Blood Thrall and Drudge death bursts: blood cells double their starting velocity, so with the code's own upward speeds (0.15 and 0.1) they fly well above the body. The old red dust barely moved. If they should stay low, cut the upward speed.
- The Morphling Polyp item's "consumed" particles were dead code: they sat in a client branch the method never reached. They now come from the server when the recipe completes, with a 0.2-block spread I chose so the six puffs do not stack on one point.
- The Circus Acclimating glow: a will-absorption glow flies from its spawn point plus its velocity back to its spawn point. To keep the old drift toward the player, it spawns 22 ticks of drift ahead (the glow's mean life) and is handed the drift reversed.
- The Jade tooltip still shows in the review shots, as in P16 and P17.
- No new player text, no payload change, no blood paid.

### Verification (round 1)

Verdict: pass. The tree is green and nothing blocking was found.

- Scope: only chunk 09 T6 (batches B, D1, D2, E) and its two review scenes are built, as the brief allows. No open NQ, conflict, UN- or late CQ- row touches it. Nothing on the DO NOT TOUCH list was edited: no lash, held form, living HUD or vat file. None of the swept sites is a manipulation (the Orb is the Living Spear's, the Echo the Masquerade armour's, the Thrall its item's, Neural Overload the Myelin Borer's and the Naeglerophaeon's, the Elytra effect the Noctifly Agaric's). No payload, lang key, network or resource file changed, and no blood is paid. The tracked diff against `refs/overnight/P18-before` (27 source files, the allow-list, the review-clients rows, `TESTING.md`, `ARCHITECTURE_STANDARDS.md`, the log) and the status compared with `P18-status-before.txt` (plus the two new review classes) match the log.
- Values, checked against the audit's B, D1, D2 and E tables: every site has the audit's particle, colour, count, scale, alpha and life. `sendParticles` speeds are halved for the particles that double their velocity and kept for the will-absorption and daemon glows; `addParticle` velocities are the code's own. The Morphic Nectar soak keeps its count of 3, as the audit's "same count" says. The Polyp's consumed branch really was dead (the method returns early on the client), so moving it to the server changes no gameplay. The Circus Acclimating fix matches `WillAbsorptionGlowParticle`, which flies from spawn plus velocity to spawn. `PossessionEvents`, `BiomeInit`, `CircusDemonstrations` and the two armour items have no vanilla particle left.
- Allow-list: 17 rows, none pending, nothing went up; Choir Keeper 5 to 4 with its reason. The ratchet pins every swap, so reverting one fails `VanillaParticleRatchetTest`. No test was weakened.
- Unit suite (`build/overnight/P18-verify1-unit.txt`): `BUILD SUCCESSFUL in 1m 39s`, 6,031 tests, 0 failures, 0 errors, 0 skipped.
- Combined GameTest run (`build/overnight/P18-verify1-gametest.txt`, log `P18-verify1-gametest-latest.log`): 1,917 tests, 3 required failures, exactly the baseline three (dialogue route fingerprint, registry ids, creative tab order).
